# Integrated competitive scorecard

> **Diátaxis Type:** Reference

Generated by `make-scorecard.py` from `dev/active/1a379447-zen3-cpu-performance/1362381c/workload-set.json`, `dev/active/1a379447-zen3-cpu-performance/1362381c/production-drift.json` and `dev/active/1a379447-zen3-cpu-performance/1362381c/gaps.json`; `scorecard.json` holds every field at full precision. The raw samples are the per-pair records of each row's `receipt.json`. Each campaign is verified from its execution log.

Row labels: `consumed` rows are evidence of the code their receipt pins, labelled with the drift class; they are never measurements of the current tree. `integrated` rows belong to the families of this issue. Speedup is baseline median time over candidate median time. Latency, throughput and batch-fill intervals are descriptive order-statistic intervals of the per-execution medians; speedup intervals are the acceptance summary's percentile bootstrap. B and C name the baseline and candidate arms. Decoder per-worker differences include any route change `selected_path` records.

Target outcome rule: per comparison, decision regressed: met; decision improved: not met; any other decision, a not-confirmatory outcome or a published ruling on the receipt: inconclusive; an unmeasured cell: unavailable. The target holds per workload: a loss to another project's measured arm on the same operation, size, consumer scope, core arm, gf2 build and gf2 path makes the cell not met, and an undecided comparison there makes a met cell inconclusive; `pairwise` keeps the cell's own comparison. Gap rule: a primary comparator-gap cell whose outcome is pass, or whose estimate exceeds the family's frozen material_gap_threshold; each keeps its outcome as evaluated.
